// frontend/src/main.jsx
import { StrictMode } from "react";
import { createRoot } from "react-dom/client";
import { BrowserRouter } from "react-router-dom";
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { AuthProvider } from "./context/AuthContext";
import { ToastProvider } from "./components/ui/Toast";
import ErrorBoundary from "./components/ErrorBoundary";
import App from "./App";
import "./index.css";
import "@fontsource/poppins/400.css";
import "@fontsource/poppins/500.css";
import "@fontsource/poppins/600.css";

// retry:false evita que un 401 de /auth/me se reintente 3 veces antes de mostrar el login.
const queryClient = new QueryClient({
  defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false, refetchOnWindowFocus: false } },
});

createRoot(document.getElementById("root")).render(
  <StrictMode>
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      <ToastProvider>
        <BrowserRouter>
          <AuthProvider>
            <ErrorBoundary>
              <App />
            </ErrorBoundary>
          </AuthProvider>
        </BrowserRouter>
      </ToastProvider>
    </QueryClientProvider>
  </StrictMode>,
);




//==========================================
// FILE: frontend\src\api\client.js
//==========================================
// frontend/src/api/client.js
import axios from "axios";

// Cliente HTTP único. withCredentials hace que el navegador envíe/reciba la cookie de sesión.
export const api = axios.create({
  baseURL: import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL ?? "/api",
  withCredentials: true,
});

// Normaliza errores: el resto de la app solo lee `error.userMessage`.
api.interceptors.response.use(
  (res) => res,
  (error) => {
    error.userMessage =
      error.response?.data?.message ?? "No se pudo conectar con el servidor";
    error.code = error.response?.data?.code;
    return Promise.reject(error);
  },
);

export default api;




//==========================================
// FILE: frontend\src\components\ErrorBoundary.jsx
//==========================================
// frontend/src/components/ErrorBoundary.jsx
import { Component } from "react";

export default class ErrorBoundary extends Component {
  state = { error: null };

  static getDerivedStateFromError(error) {
    return { error };
  }

  componentDidCatch(error, info) {
    console.error("[ErrorBoundary]", error, info?.componentStack);
  }

  render() {
    if (this.state.error) {
      return (
        <div className="rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700">
          <p className="font-semibold">
            Algo salió mal al mostrar esta pantalla
          </p>
          <p className="mt-1">{this.state.error.message}</p>
          <button
            onClick={() => this.setState({ error: null })}
            className="mt-3 rounded-lg bg-red-600 px-3 py-1.5 text-white hover:bg-red-700"
          >
            Reintentar
          </button>
        </div>
      );
    }
    return this.props.children;
  }
}




//==========================================
// FILE: frontend\src\components\ProtectedRoute.jsx
//==========================================
// frontend/src/components/ProtectedRoute.jsx
import { Navigate, Outlet, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext.jsx";

// Guard de rutas. Solo experiencia de usuario: la seguridad REAL está en el backend.
// Se usa de dos formas:
//   - Como layout:   <ProtectedRoute><AppLayout /></ProtectedRoute>  → renderiza children
//   - Como wrapper:  <ProtectedRoute roles={["admin"]} />             → renderiza <Outlet />
export default function ProtectedRoute({ roles, children }) {
  const { user, isLoading } = useAuth();
  const location = useLocation();

  if (isLoading)
    return (
      <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center text-slate-500">
        Cargando…
      </div>
    );
  if (!user) return <Navigate to="/login" replace state={{ from: location }} />;
  if (roles && !roles.includes(user.rol))
    return <Navigate to="/admin/dashboard" replace />;
  return children ?? <Outlet />;
}




//==========================================
// FILE: frontend\src\components\layout\AppLayout.jsx
//==========================================
// frontend/src/components/layout/AppLayout.jsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { NavLink, Outlet, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import {
  LayoutDashboard,
  Car,
  ArrowLeftRight,
  CalendarCheck,
  Settings,
  Tags,
  Banknote,
  Users,
  BarChart3,
  LogOut,
  Menu,
  UserCircle,
  DoorClosed,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useAuth } from "../../context/AuthContext";
import { TurnoProvider, useTurno } from "../turnos/TurnoProvider";
import Badge from "../ui/Badge";
import ErrorBoundary from "../ui/ErrorBoundary";

const MENU = [
  { to: "/admin/dashboard", icon: LayoutDashboard, label: "Dashboard" },
  { to: "/admin/vehiculos", icon: Car, label: "Vehículos" },
  {
    to: "/admin/ingreso-salida",
    icon: ArrowLeftRight,
    label: "Ingreso / Salida",
  },
  { to: "/admin/mensualidades", icon: CalendarCheck, label: "Mensualidades" },
  {
    to: "/admin/configuracion",
    icon: Settings,
    label: "Configuración",
    admin: true,
  },
  {
    to: "/admin/tipos-vehiculos",
    icon: Tags,
    label: "Tipos de Vehículos",
    admin: true,
  },
  { to: "/admin/tarifas", icon: Banknote, label: "Tarifas", admin: true },
  { to: "/admin/usuarios", icon: Users, label: "Usuarios", admin: true },
  { to: "/admin/reportes", icon: BarChart3, label: "Reportes", admin: true },
];

function Shell() {
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();
  const { abierto, abrirModal } = useTurno();
  const { pathname } = useLocation();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const items = MENU.filter((m) => !m.admin || user?.rol === "admin");

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50">
      {open && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-30 bg-black/40 lg:hidden"
          onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
        />
      )}
      <aside
        className={`fixed inset-y-0 left-0 z-40 flex w-64 flex-col bg-brand-600 text-white transition-transform lg:translate-x-0 ${open ? "translate-x-0" : "-translate-x-full"}`}
      >
        <div className="p-6 text-center">
          <div className="mx-auto flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white text-xl font-semibold text-brand-600">
            PS
          </div>
          <h5 className="mt-3 font-medium">ParkSystem</h5>
        </div>
        <nav className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto py-2">
          {items.map(({ to, icon: Icon, label }) => (
            <NavLink
              key={to}
              to={to}
              onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
              className={({ isActive }) =>
                `flex items-center gap-3 px-6 py-3 text-sm transition-colors ${isActive ? "bg-white/10 text-white" : "text-white/80 hover:bg-white/10 hover:text-white"}`
              }
            >
              <Icon size={18} />
              {label}
            </NavLink>
          ))}
        </nav>
        <div className="border-t border-white/10 p-4">
          <button
            onClick={logout}
            className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 px-2 py-2 text-sm text-white/80 hover:text-white"
          >
            <LogOut size={18} />
            Cerrar Sesión
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      <div className="lg:pl-64">
        <header className="sticky top-0 z-20 flex items-center justify-between bg-white px-4 py-3 shadow-sm">
          <button
            className="rounded p-2 text-brand-600 hover:bg-slate-100 lg:hidden"
            onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
            aria-label="Abrir menú"
          >
            <Menu size={20} />
          </button>
          <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-3">
            <Badge tone={abierto ? "green" : "gray"}>
              {abierto ? "Turno abierto" : "Turno cerrado"}
            </Badge>
            <button
              onClick={() => abrirModal(abierto ? "cierre" : "apertura")}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-red-300 px-3 py-1 text-sm text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50"
            >
              <DoorClosed size={14} />
              {abierto ? "Cerrar turno" : "Abrir turno"}
            </button>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
              <UserCircle size={20} />
              {user?.nombre ?? "Usuario"}
            </span>
          </div>
        </header>
        <main className="p-4 sm:p-6">
          <ErrorBoundary key={pathname}>
            <Outlet />
          </ErrorBoundary>
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function AppLayout() {
  return (
    <TurnoProvider>
      <Shell />
    </TurnoProvider>
  );
}




//==========================================
// FILE: frontend\src\components\movimientos\IngresoForm.jsx
//==========================================
// components/movimientos/IngresoForm.jsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../../context/AuthContext.jsx";
import {
  useRegistrarIngreso,
  useTiposActivos,
} from "../../hooks/useMovimientos.js";
import { formatDateTime } from "../../utils/format.js";
import Button from "../ui/Button.jsx";
import { useTurno } from "../turnos/TurnoProvider";

export default function IngresoForm({ onDone }) {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { abierto, requireTurno } = useTurno();
  const { data: tipos = [] } = useTiposActivos();
  const ingreso = useRegistrarIngreso();
  const [placa, setPlaca] = useState("");
  const [tipoId, setTipoId] = useState(null);

  const onSubmit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!requireTurno()) return; // sin turno abierto: se abre el modal de apertura
    ingreso.mutate(
      { placa, id_tipo: tipoId },
      {
        onSuccess: (d) => {
          setPlaca("");
          onDone?.(d.movimientoId);
        },
      },
    );
  };

  const c = ingreso.data; // comprobante del último ingreso

  return (
    <section className="rounded-xl bg-white p-6 shadow">
      <h2 className="mb-4 text-lg font-semibold">Ingreso rápido</h2>
      <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="space-y-4">
        <input
          value={placa}
          onChange={(e) => setPlaca(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
          required
          placeholder="ABC123"
          className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2 uppercase"
        />
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {tipos.map((t) => (
            <button
              key={t.id_tipo}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setTipoId(t.id_tipo)}
              className={`rounded-lg border-2 px-4 py-2 text-sm ${tipoId === t.id_tipo ? "border-brand-600 bg-brand-600 text-white" : "border-gray-300"}`}
            >
              {t.nombre}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        {ingreso.isError && (
          <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-red-600">
            {ingreso.error.userMessage}
          </p>
        )}
        <Button
          type="submit"
          variant="success"
          loading={ingreso.isPending}
          disabled={!placa || !tipoId || !abierto}
          className="w-full"
        >
          Ingresar
        </Button>
      </form>

      {c && (
        <div className="mt-4 rounded-lg bg-green-50 p-4 text-sm">
          <p>
            Movimiento <b>#{c.movimientoId}</b> · Placa <b>{c.placa}</b> ·{" "}
            {c.tipo}
          </p>
          <p>
            Entrada: {formatDateTime(c.fechaEntrada, user.config?.zona_horaria)}
          </p>
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}




//==========================================
// FILE: frontend\src\components\movimientos\PagoModal.jsx
//==========================================
// frontend/src/components/movimientos/PagoModal.jsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../../context/AuthContext.jsx";
import { formatMoney } from "../../utils/format.js";
import Button from "../ui/Button.jsx";

const METODOS = ["efectivo", "tarjeta", "QR", "transferencia"];

export default function PagoModal({
  factura,
  onConfirm,
  onCancel,
  loading,
  error,
}) {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const moneda = user.config?.moneda ?? "COP";

  // Punto de partida: UNA fila de efectivo con el total exacto.
  // El cajero debe editar este monto si el cliente entregó más.
  const [pagos, setPagos] = useState([
    { metodo_pago: "efectivo", monto: factura.total },
  ]);

  const recibido = pagos.reduce((s, p) => s + Number(p.monto || 0), 0);
  const diff = recibido - factura.total;

  const set = (i, patch) =>
    setPagos((ps) => ps.map((p, j) => (j === i ? { ...p, ...patch } : p)));

  const cubrirConTotal = () => {
    // Rellena la primera fila con el total exacto en efectivo.
    // Útil cuando el cajero ya había editado el campo y quiere volver al default.
    const otros = pagos.slice(1).reduce((s, p) => s + Number(p.monto || 0), 0);
    const faltante = Math.max(0, factura.total - otros);
    set(0, { metodo_pago: "efectivo", monto: faltante });
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/40 p-4"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
    >
      <div className="w-full max-w-md space-y-4 rounded-xl bg-white p-6 shadow-xl">
        <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Registrar pago</h3>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg bg-slate-50 px-3 py-2">
          <span className="text-sm text-slate-600">Total a pagar</span>
          <b className="text-lg">{formatMoney(factura.total, moneda)}</b>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-2">
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
            Escribe el monto que entrega el cliente por cada medio de pago. Si
            entrega más del total, el sobrante aparece como vuelto.
          </p>
          {pagos.map((p, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="flex gap-2">
              <select
                value={p.metodo_pago}
                onChange={(e) => set(i, { metodo_pago: e.target.value })}
                className="rounded-lg border px-2 py-1"
              >
                {METODOS.map((m) => (
                  <option key={m}>{m}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
              <input
                type="number"
                min="0"
                step="0.01"
                value={p.monto}
                onChange={(e) => set(i, { monto: e.target.value })}
                placeholder="0.00"
                className="w-full rounded-lg border px-2 py-1"
              />
              {pagos.length > 1 && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setPagos((ps) => ps.filter((_, j) => j !== i))}
                  aria-label="Quitar"
                  className="rounded px-2 text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100"
                >
                  ×
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            className="text-sm text-brand-600 hover:underline"
            onClick={() =>
              setPagos((ps) => [...ps, { metodo_pago: "tarjeta", monto: "" }])
            }
          >
            + Agregar medio de pago
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="text-sm text-slate-500 hover:underline"
            onClick={cubrirConTotal}
          >
            Rellenar con el total
          </button>
        </div>

        <div
          className={`rounded-lg p-3 text-sm ${diff >= 0 ? "bg-green-50 text-green-700" : "bg-red-50 text-red-700"}`}
        >
          Recibido: <b>{formatMoney(recibido, moneda)}</b> ·{" "}
          {diff >= 0 ? "Vuelto" : "Falta"}:{" "}
          <b>{formatMoney(Math.abs(diff), moneda)}</b>
        </div>

        {error && (
          <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-red-600">
            {error}
          </p>
        )}

        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
          <Button variant="outline" onClick={onCancel}>
            Cancelar
          </Button>
          <Button
            variant="success"
            loading={loading}
            disabled={diff < 0}
            onClick={() =>
              onConfirm(
                pagos
                  .map((p) => ({ ...p, monto: Number(p.monto) }))
                  .filter((p) => p.monto > 0),
              )
            }
          >
            Confirmar pagos
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}




//==========================================
// FILE: frontend\src\components\movimientos\SalidaPanel.jsx
//==========================================
// components/movimientos/SalidaPanel.jsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../../context/AuthContext.jsx";
import {
  useCalcularSalida,
  useConfirmarSalida,
} from "../../hooks/useMovimientos.js";
import { formatDateTime, formatMoney } from "../../utils/format.js";
import Button from "../ui/Button.jsx";
import PagoModal from "./PagoModal.jsx";
import { useTurno } from "../turnos/TurnoProvider";

export default function SalidaPanel() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { requireTurno } = useTurno();
  const tz = user.config?.zona_horaria;
  const [placa, setPlaca] = useState("");
  const [factura, setFactura] = useState(null);
  const [final, setFinal] = useState(null);
  const calcular = useCalcularSalida();
  const confirmar = useConfirmarSalida();

  const onCalcular = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setFinal(null);
    calcular.mutate(placa, { onSuccess: setFactura });
  };

  const onConfirm = (pagos) => {
    if (!requireTurno()) return; // sin turno abierto: se abre el modal de apertura
    confirmar.mutate(
      { placa: factura.placa, pagos },
      {
        onSuccess: (data) => {
          setFinal(data);
          setFactura(null);
          setPlaca("");
        },
      },
    );
  };

  return (
    <section className="rounded-xl bg-white p-6 shadow">
      <h2 className="mb-4 text-lg font-semibold">Salida y cobro</h2>
      <form onSubmit={onCalcular} className="flex gap-2">
        <input
          value={placa}
          onChange={(e) => setPlaca(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
          required
          placeholder="ABC123"
          className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2 uppercase"
        />
        <Button type="submit" variant="danger" loading={calcular.isPending}>
          Calcular
        </Button>
      </form>
      {calcular.isError && (
        <p role="alert" className="mt-2 text-sm text-red-600">
          {calcular.error.userMessage}
        </p>
      )}

      {final && (
        <div className="mt-4 rounded-lg bg-green-50 p-4 text-sm">
          <p>
            Salida registrada: <b>{final.placa}</b> · Total{" "}
            {formatMoney(final.total, user.config?.moneda)}
          </p>
          <p>
            Vuelto: {formatMoney(final.vuelto, user.config?.moneda)} · Salida:{" "}
            {formatDateTime(final.fechaSalida, tz)}
          </p>
        </div>
      )}

      {factura && (
        <PagoModal
          factura={factura}
          loading={confirmar.isPending}
          error={confirmar.error?.userMessage}
          onCancel={() => setFactura(null)}
          onConfirm={onConfirm}
        />
      )}
    </section>
  );
}




//==========================================
// FILE: frontend\src\components\tickets\ComprobantePanel.jsx
//==========================================
// frontend/src/components/tickets/ComprobantePanel.jsx
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from "react";
import { Printer, X } from "lucide-react";
import { useGet } from "../../hooks/useApi";
import { useFormat } from "../../hooks/useEmpresa";
import { printHtml, ticketDocument } from "../../utils/print";
import { buildTicketHtml } from "./ticketHtml";
import Button from "../ui/Button";

export const useTicket = (id) =>
  useGet(["ticket", id], `/movimientos/${id}/ticket`, {
    enabled: !!id,
    staleTime: 60_000,
  });

export default function ComprobantePanel({
  id,
  tipo,
  autoPrint = false,
  onClose,
}) {
  const { data, isLoading, error } = useTicket(id);
  const fmt = useFormat();
  const impreso = useRef(false);
  const titulo = tipo === "INGRESO" ? "Comprobante de Ingreso" : "Factura";

  const preview = useMemo(
    () =>
      data
        ? ticketDocument(buildTicketHtml(data, tipo, fmt), {
            widthMm: 80,
            title: titulo,
          })
        : "",
    [data, tipo, fmt, titulo],
  );
  const imprimir = (widthMm) =>
    printHtml(buildTicketHtml(data, tipo, fmt), { widthMm, title: titulo }); // hora de impresión fresca

  // La factura de salida se imprime sola al confirmar el pago (80 mm), como en el original.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (autoPrint && data && !impreso.current) {
      impreso.current = true;
      imprimir(80);
    }
  }, [autoPrint, data]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps

  return (
    <div className="mt-4 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 p-3">
      <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between">
        <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">{titulo}</h3>
        <button
          onClick={onClose}
          aria-label="Cerrar"
          className="rounded p-1 text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100"
        >
          <X size={16} />
        </button>
      </div>
      {isLoading && (
        <p className="py-6 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
          Generando comprobante…
        </p>
      )}
      {error && (
        <p className="py-6 text-center text-sm text-red-600">
          {error.userMessage}
        </p>
      )}
      {data && (
        <>
          <iframe
            title="Vista previa del ticket"
            srcDoc={preview}
            className="h-96 w-full rounded border border-slate-200 bg-white"
          />
          <div className="mt-2 flex gap-2">
            <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => imprimir(58)}>
              <Printer size={14} className="mr-1" />
              Imprimir 58 mm
            </Button>
            <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => imprimir(80)}>
              <Printer size={14} className="mr-1" />
              Imprimir 80 mm
            </Button>
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// frontend/src/components/tickets/recibo.jsx
import { renderToStaticMarkup } from "react-dom/server";
import api from "../../api/client";
import { printHtml } from "../../utils/print";
import { fmtYmd } from "../../utils/ymd";

const LOGO = `${import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL ?? "/api"}/empresa/logo`;
const metodo = (m) =>
  ({
    efectivo: "Efectivo",
    tarjeta: "Tarjeta",
    QR: "QR",
    transferencia: "Transferencia",
  })[m] ?? m;

export function ReciboMensualidad({ data, fmtMoney, fmtDate, impreso }) {
  const { empresa: e, mensualidad: m, pago: p } = data;
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="c">
        {e.tiene_logo && <img src={LOGO} alt="" />}
        <div className="b">{e.nombre}</div>
        <div>NIT: {e.nit}</div>
        <div>{[e.direccion, e.telefono].filter(Boolean).join(" - ")}</div>
        <hr />
        <div className="b">PAGO MENSUALIDAD</div>
      </div>
      <div>
        Mensualidad: <span className="b">#{m.id}</span>
      </div>
      <div>
        Placa: <span className="b">{m.placa}</span>
      </div>
      <div>
        Titular: <span className="b">{m.titular}</span>
      </div>
      <div>
        Periodo:{" "}
        <span className="b">
          {fmtYmd(p.periodo_desde)} - {fmtYmd(p.periodo_hasta)}
        </span>
      </div>
      <div>
        Método: <span className="b">{metodo(p.metodo_pago)}</span>
      </div>
      <div>
        Monto: <span className="b">{fmtMoney(p.monto)}</span>
      </div>
      {p.referencia && (
        <div>
          Referencia: <span className="b">{p.referencia}</span>
        </div>
      )}
      <div>Fecha: {fmtDate(p.fecha_pago)}</div>
      <div>Atendido por: {data.atendido}</div>
      <div>Impreso: {impreso}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

export async function imprimirRecibo(idMens, idPago, fmt, widthMm = 80) {
  const { data } = await api.get(
    `/mensualidades/${idMens}/pagos/${idPago}/recibo`,
  );
  const html = renderToStaticMarkup(
    <ReciboMensualidad
      data={data.data}
      fmtMoney={fmt.fmtMoney}
      fmtDate={fmt.fmtDate}
      impreso={fmt.fmtDate(new Date().toISOString())}
    />,
  );
  return printHtml(html, { widthMm, title: "Recibo de pago" });
}

// FILE: frontend\src\components\tickets\Ticket.jsx

// frontend/src/components/tickets/Ticket.jsx
// Se renderiza con renderToStaticMarkup: React escapa todos los datos (placa, empresa, etc.).
import { barcodeSvg } from "../../utils/barcode";

const LOGO = `${import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL ?? "/api"}/empresa/logo`;
const hhmm = (t) => String(t ?? "").slice(0, 5);
const metodo = (m) =>
  ({ efectivo: "Efectivo", tarjeta: "Tarjeta", QR: "QR" })[m] ?? m;

export default function Ticket({ tipo, data, fmtMoney, fmtDate, impreso }) {
  const {
    empresa: e,
    config: c,
    movimiento: m,
    vehiculo: v,
    tarifa: t,
    tiempo,
    pagos,
    atendido,
  } = data;
  const salida = tipo === "SALIDA";
  const horario = c.operacion_24h
    ? "24 horas"
    : `${hhmm(c.horario_apertura)} - ${hhmm(c.horario_cierre)}`;

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="c">
        {e.tiene_logo && <img src={LOGO} alt="" />}
        <div className="b">{e.nombre}</div>
        <div>NIT: {e.nit}</div>
        <div>{[e.direccion, e.telefono].filter(Boolean).join(" - ")}</div>
        <div>
          Horario: <span className="b">{horario}</span>
        </div>
        <hr />
        <div className="b">{tipo}</div>
      </div>

      <div>
        Movimiento: <span className="b">#{m.id}</span>
      </div>
      <div>
        Placa: <span className="b">{v.placa}</span>
      </div>
      <div>
        Tipo: <span className="b">{v.tipo}</span>
      </div>
      <div>
        Entrada: <span className="b">{fmtDate(m.fecha_entrada)}</span>
      </div>
      {salida && (
        <div>
          Salida: <span className="b">{fmtDate(m.fecha_salida)}</span>
        </div>
      )}
      {salida && (
        <div>
          Tiempo:{" "}
          <span className="b">
            {tiempo.dias}d {tiempo.horas}h {tiempo.minutos}m
          </span>
        </div>
      )}
      <hr />
      <div>Tarifas</div>
      <div>
        Minuto: <span className="b">{fmtMoney(t.valor_minuto)}</span>
      </div>
      <div>
        Hora: <span className="b">{fmtMoney(t.valor_hora)}</span>
      </div>
      <div>
        Día: <span className="b">{fmtMoney(t.valor_dia_completo)}</span>
      </div>
      <hr />

      {salida && (
        <>
          <div className="row b">
            <span>Total a pagar</span>
            <span>{fmtMoney(m.total)}</span>
          </div>
          {pagos.length > 0 && <div className="b">Pagos</div>}
          {pagos.map((p, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="row">
              <span>{metodo(p.metodo_pago)}</span>
              <span>{fmtMoney(p.monto)}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
          {data.vuelto > 0 && (
            <>
              <div className="row">
                <span>Recibido</span>
                <span>{fmtMoney(data.recibido)}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="row b">
                <span>Vuelto</span>
                <span>{fmtMoney(data.vuelto)}</span>
              </div>
            </>
          )}
          <hr />
        </>
      )}

      <div>Atendido por: {salida ? atendido.salida : atendido.entrada}</div>
      <div>Fecha impresión: {impreso}</div>
      {!salida && (
        <div dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: barcodeSvg(v.placa) }} />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

  return (

      </div>
      <div>

      <div>
        Base inicial: <span className="b">{fmtMoney(r.base)}</span>

      <div className="row">
        <span>QR</span>
        <span>{fmtMoney(r.conteo.qr)}</span>
      </div>
      <div className="row b">
        <span>Total</span>
        <span>{fmtMoney(r.conteo.total)}</span>
      </div>
      <hr />
      <div className="b">Sistema</div>
      <div className="row">
        <span>Efectivo</span>
        <span>{fmtMoney(r.esperado.efectivo)}</span>
      </div>
      <div className="row">
        <span>Tarjeta</span>
        <span>{fmtMoney(r.esperado.tarjeta)}</span>
      </div>
      <div className="row">
        <span>QR</span>
        <span>{fmtMoney(r.esperado.qr)}</span>
      </div>
      <div className="row b">
        <span>Total</span>
        <span>{fmtMoney(r.esperado.total)}</span>
      </div>
      {r.esperado.transferencia > 0 && (
        <div>
          Transferencias (fuera de caja): {fmtMoney(r.esperado.transferencia)}
        </div>
      )}
      <hr />
      <div className="b">
        {cuadra
          ? "Caja cuadra"
          : dif < 0
            ? `FALTANTE EN CAJA: ${fmtMoney(Math.abs(dif))}`
            : `SOBRANTE EN CAJA: ${fmtMoney(dif)}`}
      </div>
      {r.observacion && <div>Obs.: {r.observacion}</div>}
    </div>
  );
}




//==========================================
// FILE: frontend\src\components\turnos\TurnoModal.jsx
//==========================================
// frontend/src/components/turnos/TurnoModal.jsx
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { renderToStaticMarkup } from "react-dom/server";
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Printer } from "lucide-react";
import { api } from "../../api/client";
import { useGet } from "../../hooks/useApi";
import { useEmpresa, useFormat } from "../../hooks/useEmpresa";
import { printHtml } from "../../utils/print";
import Modal from "../ui/Modal";
import Button from "../ui/Button";
import { Field, FormError, inputCls } from "../ui/Field";
import { useToast } from "../ui/Toast";
import CierreTicket from "./CierreTicket";

const num = (v) => Number(v || 0);

function Apertura({ onClose }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [base, setBase] = useState("");
  const [obs, setObs] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  async function submit(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      await api.post("/turnos/abrir", {
        base_inicial: num(base),
        observacion_apertura: obs || undefined,
      });
      await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["turno"] });
      toast.success("Turno abierto correctamente");
      onClose();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.userMessage);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <Modal
      open
      onClose={onClose}
      title="Turno de Caja — Apertura"
      footer={
        <>
          <Button variant="outline" onClick={onClose}>
            Cerrar
          </Button>
          <Button type="submit" form="apertura" loading={busy}>
            Abrir turno
          </Button>
        </>
      }
    >
      <form id="apertura" onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-3">
        <FormError message={error} />
        <Field label="Base inicial">
          <input
            type="number"
            min="0"
            step="0.01"
            className={inputCls}
            value={base}
            onChange={(e) => setBase(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="0.00"
            autoFocus
          />
        </Field>
        <Field label="Observación (opcional)">
          <input
            maxLength={255}
            className={inputCls}
            value={obs}
            onChange={(e) => setObs(e.target.value)}
          />
        </Field>
        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
          Debes abrir turno para poder registrar ingresos y salidas.
        </p>
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
}

function Cierre({ onClose }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const { fmtMoney, fmtDate } = useFormat();
  const { data: empresa } = useEmpresa();
  const { data: res } = useGet(["turno", "resumen"], "/turnos/resumen", {
    staleTime: 0,
    gcTime: 0,
  });
  const [v, setV] = useState({ ef: "", ta: "", qr: "", obs: "" });
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [cerrado, setCerrado] = useState(null);
  const set = (k) => (e) => setV({ ...v, [k]: e.target.value });

  // Pre-carga los totales esperados por método de pago cuando el resumen llega.
  // No sobreescribe lo que el cajero ya haya escrito.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!res?.totales) return;
    setV((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      ef: prev.ef === "" ? String(res.totales.efectivo) : prev.ef,
      ta: prev.ta === "" ? String(res.totales.tarjeta) : prev.ta,
      qr: prev.qr === "" ? String(res.totales.qr) : prev.qr,
    }));
  }, [res?.totales]);

  const total = num(v.ef) + num(v.ta) + num(v.qr);
  const esperado = res?.totales;
  const diff = esperado ? Math.round((total - esperado.total) * 100) / 100 : 0;

  const imprimir = (data, widthMm) =>
    printHtml(
      renderToStaticMarkup(
        <CierreTicket
          empresa={empresa}
          r={data}
          fmtMoney={fmtMoney}
          ahora={fmtDate(new Date().toISOString())}
        />,
      ),
      { widthMm, title: "Cierre de turno" },
    );

  async function submit(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      const { data } = await api.post("/turnos/cerrar", {
        total_efectivo: num(v.ef),
        total_tarjeta: num(v.ta),
        total_qr: num(v.qr),
        observacion_cierre: v.obs || undefined,
      });
      await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["turno"] });
      setCerrado(data.data);
      // Imprime automáticamente al cerrar (80 mm por defecto).
      imprimir(data.data, 80);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.userMessage);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  if (cerrado) {
    const dif = cerrado.diferencia;
    return (
      <Modal
        open
        onClose={onClose}
        title="Turno cerrado"
        footer={
          <>
            <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => imprimir(cerrado, 58)}>
              <Printer size={14} className="mr-1" />
              Imprimir 58 mm
            </Button>
            <Button onClick={() => imprimir(cerrado, 80)}>
              <Printer size={14} className="mr-1" />
              Imprimir 80 mm
            </Button>
            <Button variant="outline" onClick={onClose}>
              Cerrar
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <p
          className={`mb-3 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm ${
            Math.abs(dif) < 0.01
              ? "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700"
              : dif < 0
                ? "bg-red-50 text-red-700"
                : "bg-amber-50 text-amber-800"
          }`}
        >
          {Math.abs(dif) < 0.01
            ? "Turno cerrado sin diferencias. Caja cuadra."
            : dif < 0
              ? `Falta dinero en caja: ${fmtMoney(Math.abs(dif))}.`
              : `Sobra dinero en caja: ${fmtMoney(dif)}.`}
        </p>
        <dl className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-sm">
          <dt className="text-slate-500">Conteo</dt>
          <dd className="text-right font-medium">
            {fmtMoney(cerrado.conteo.total)}
          </dd>
          <dt className="text-slate-500">Sistema</dt>
          <dd className="text-right font-medium">
            {fmtMoney(cerrado.esperado.total)}
          </dd>
          <dt className="text-slate-500">Tickets</dt>
          <dd className="text-right font-medium">{cerrado.stats.total}</dd>
        </dl>
      </Modal>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Modal
      open
      onClose={onClose}
      title="Turno de Caja — Cierre"
      footer={
        <>
          <Button variant="outline" onClick={onClose}>
            Cancelar
          </Button>
          <Button variant="success" type="submit" form="cierre" loading={busy}>
            Cerrar e imprimir
          </Button>
        </>
      }
    >
      <form id="cierre" onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-3">
        <FormError message={error} />

        {esperado?.transferencia > 0 && (
          <p className="rounded bg-amber-50 px-2 py-1 text-xs text-amber-800">
            <b>No incluyas esta cifra en Efectivo/Tarjeta/QR.</b> Transferencias
            de mensualidades en este turno: {fmtMoney(esperado.transferencia)}.
            No pasan por caja física.
          </p>
        )}

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <Field label="Efectivo">
            <input
              type="number"
              min="0"
              step="0.01"
              className={inputCls}
              value={v.ef}
              onChange={set("ef")}
              placeholder="0.00"
              autoFocus
            />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Tarjeta">
            <input
              type="number"
              min="0"
              step="0.01"
              className={inputCls}
              value={v.ta}
              onChange={set("ta")}
              placeholder="0.00"
            />
          </Field>
          <Field label="QR">
            <input
              type="number"
              min="0"
              step="0.01"
              className={inputCls}
              value={v.qr}
              onChange={set("qr")}
              placeholder="0.00"
            />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Total">
            <input readOnly className={inputCls} value={total.toFixed(2)} />
          </Field>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-between gap-2 text-sm">
          <span>
            Sistema (esperado):{" "}
            <strong>{esperado ? fmtMoney(esperado.total) : "…"}</strong>
          </span>
          <span>
            Diferencia:{" "}
            <strong
              className={
                Math.abs(diff) < 0.01
                  ? "text-emerald-600"
                  : diff < 0
                    ? "text-red-600"
                    : "text-amber-700"
              }
            >
              {fmtMoney(diff)}
              {Math.abs(diff) >= 0.01 && (
                <span className="ml-1 font-normal">
                  ({diff < 0 ? "falta" : "sobra"})
                </span>
              )}
            </strong>
          </span>
        </div>

        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
          La diferencia es <b>conteo menos sistema</b>. Negativa significa que
          falta dinero en caja; positiva, que sobra.
        </p>

        <Field label="Observación de cierre">
          <input
            maxLength={255}
            className={inputCls}
            value={v.obs}
            onChange={set("obs")}
          />
        </Field>
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
}

export default function TurnoModal({ modo, onClose }) {
  return modo === "cierre" ? (
    <Cierre onClose={onClose} />
  ) : (
    <Apertura onClose={onClose} />
  );
}




//==========================================
// FILE: frontend\src\components\turnos\TurnoProvider.jsx
//==========================================
// frontend/src/components/turnos/TurnoProvider.jsx
import {
  createContext,
  useCallback,
  useContext,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useRef,
  useState,
} from "react";
import { useGet } from "../../hooks/useApi";
import TurnoModal from "./TurnoModal";

const Ctx = createContext(null);
export const useTurno = () => useContext(Ctx);

export function TurnoProvider({ children }) {
  const { data: turno, isSuccess } = useGet(
    ["turno", "actual"],
    "/turnos/actual",
  );
  const [modal, setModal] = useState(null); // "apertura" | "cierre" | null
  const preguntado = useRef(false);

  // Igual que el original: al entrar sin turno abierto se ofrece abrirlo.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isSuccess && !turno && !preguntado.current) {
      preguntado.current = true;
      setModal("apertura");
    }
  }, [isSuccess, turno]);

  // Las acciones críticas (ingreso, salida, pago de mensualidad) llaman a esto antes de enviar.
  // El servidor también lo exige: este chequeo solo evita el viaje de ida y vuelta.
  const requireTurno = useCallback(() => {
    if (turno) return true;
    setModal("apertura");
    return false;
  }, [turno]);

  const value = useMemo(
    () => ({
      turno: turno ?? null,
      abierto: !!turno,
      requireTurno,
      abrirModal: setModal,
    }),
    [turno, requireTurno],
  );
  return (
    <Ctx.Provider value={value}>
      {children}
      {modal && <TurnoModal modo={modal} onClose={() => setModal(null)} />}
    </Ctx.Provider>
  );
}

// Componente de UI reutilizable. Variantes definidas UNA vez => consistencia en toda la app.
const variants = {
  primary: "bg-brand-600 text-white hover:bg-brand-700",
  success: "bg-green-600 text-white hover:bg-green-700",
  danger: "bg-red-600 text-white hover:bg-red-700",
  outline: "border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-50",
};

export default function Button({
  variant = "primary",
  loading = false,
  className = "",
  children,
  ...props
}) {
  return (
    <button
      {...props}
      disabled={loading || props.disabled}
      className={`inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition
        disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60 ${variants[variant]} ${className}`}
    >
      {loading && (
        <span className="size-4 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-white/40 border-t-white" />
      )}
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

//==========================================
// FILE: frontend\src\components\ui\DataTable.jsx
//==========================================
// frontend/src/components/ui/DataTable.jsx
// Reemplaza DataTables/jQuery: búsqueda, orden por columna y paginación en cliente.
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { ChevronUp, ChevronDown } from "lucide-react";

// columns: { key, header, value?(row), render?(row), sortable?, searchable?, className? }
export default function DataTable({
  columns,
  rows = [],
  loading,
  empty = "Sin registros",
  searchable = true,
  pageSize = 10,
  defaultSort,
  toolbar,
}) {
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const [sort, setSort] = useState(defaultSort ?? null);
  const [page, setPage] = useState(0);
  const get = (c, r) => (c.value ? c.value(r) : r[c.key]);

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const s = q.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!s) return rows;
    return rows.filter((r) =>
      columns.some(
        (c) =>
          c.searchable !== false &&
          String(get(c, r) ?? "")
            .toLowerCase()
            .includes(s),
      ),
    );
  }, [rows, q, columns]);

  const sorted = useMemo(() => {
    const c = sort && columns.find((x) => x.key === sort.key);
    if (!c) return filtered;
    return [...filtered].sort((a, b) => {
      const x = get(c, a),
        y = get(c, b);
      const d =
        typeof x === "number" && typeof y === "number"
          ? x - y
          : String(x ?? "").localeCompare(String(y ?? ""), "es", {
              numeric: true,
            });
      return sort.dir === "asc" ? d : -d;
    });
  }, [filtered, sort, columns]);

  const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(sorted.length / pageSize));
  const cur = Math.min(page, pages - 1);
  const slice = sorted.slice(cur * pageSize, (cur + 1) * pageSize);
  const toggle = (k) =>
    setSort((s) =>
      s?.key === k
        ? { key: k, dir: s.dir === "asc" ? "desc" : "asc" }
        : { key: k, dir: "asc" },
    );

  return (
    <div>
      {(searchable || toolbar) && (
        <div className="mb-3 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">{toolbar}</div>
          {searchable && (
            <input
              value={q}
              onChange={(e) => {
                setQ(e.target.value);
                setPage(0);
              }}
              placeholder="Buscar..."
              className="w-56 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm focus:border-brand-600 focus:outline-none"
            />
          )}
        </div>
      )}
      <div className="overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
          <thead>
            <tr className="border-b border-slate-200 text-slate-600">
              {columns.map((c) => (
                <th
                  key={c.key}
                  className={`px-3 py-2 font-semibold ${c.className ?? ""}`}
                >
                  {c.sortable === false ? (
                    c.header
                  ) : (
                    <button
                      onClick={() => toggle(c.key)}
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 hover:text-brand-600"
                    >
                      {c.header}
                      {sort?.key === c.key &&
                        (sort.dir === "asc" ? (
                          <ChevronUp size={14} />
                        ) : (
                          <ChevronDown size={14} />
                        ))}
                    </button>
                  )}
                </th>
              ))}
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {loading ? (
              <tr>
                <td
                  colSpan={columns.length}
                  className="px-3 py-8 text-center text-slate-500"
                >
                  Cargando...
                </td>
              </tr>
            ) : slice.length === 0 ? (
              <tr>
                <td
                  colSpan={columns.length}
                  className="px-3 py-8 text-center text-slate-500"
                >
                  {empty}
                </td>
              </tr>
            ) : (
              slice.map((r, i) => (
                <tr
                  key={r.id ?? r.id_vehiculo ?? r.id_tipo ?? r.id_usuario ?? i}
                  className="border-b border-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-50"
                >
                  {columns.map((c) => (
                    <td
                      key={c.key}
                      className={`px-3 py-2 ${c.className ?? ""}`}
                    >
                      {c.render ? c.render(r) : get(c, r)}
                    </td>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              ))
            )}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-slate-500">
        <span>{sorted.length} registro(s)</span>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <button
            disabled={cur === 0}
            onClick={() => setPage(cur - 1)}
            className="rounded border border-slate-300 px-2 py-1 disabled:opacity-40"
          >
            Anterior
          </button>
          <span>
            Página {cur + 1} de {pages}
          </span>
          <button
            disabled={cur >= pages - 1}
            onClick={() => setPage(cur + 1)}
            className="rounded border border-slate-300 px-2 py-1 disabled:opacity-40"
          >
            Siguiente
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}




//==========================================
// FILE: frontend\src\components\ui\ErrorBoundary.jsx
//==========================================
// frontend/src/components/ui/ErrorBoundary.jsx
import { Component } from "react";

export default class ErrorBoundary extends Component {
  state = { error: null };

  static getDerivedStateFromError(error) {
    return { error };
  }

  componentDidCatch(error, info) {
    console.error("[ErrorBoundary]", error, info?.componentStack);
  }

  render() {
    if (this.state.error) {
      return (
        <div className="rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700">
          <p className="font-semibold">
            Algo salió mal al mostrar esta pantalla
          </p>
          <p className="mt-1">{this.state.error.message}</p>
          <button
            onClick={() => this.setState({ error: null })}
            className="mt-3 rounded-lg bg-red-600 px-3 py-1.5 text-white hover:bg-red-700"
          >
            Reintentar
          </button>
        </div>
      );
    }
    return this.props.children;
  }
}

// frontend/src/components/ui/Field.jsx
export const inputCls =
  "w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm focus:border-brand-600 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-600/20 disabled:bg-slate-100 disabled:text-slate-400";

export function Field({ label, hint, className = "", children }) {
  return (
    <label className={`block ${className}`}>
      <span className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
        {label}
      </span>
      {children}
      {hint && (
        <span className="mt-1 block text-xs text-slate-500">{hint}</span>
      )}
    </label>
  );
}

export const FormError = ({ message }) =>
  message ? (
    <div
      role="alert"
      className="mb-3 rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-red-700"
    >
      {message}
    </div>
  ) : null;




//==========================================
// FILE: frontend\src\components\ui\Modal.jsx
//==========================================
// frontend/src/components/ui/Modal.jsx
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { X } from "lucide-react";

const sizes = {
  sm: "max-w-sm",
  md: "max-w-lg",
  lg: "max-w-3xl",
  xl: "max-w-5xl",
};

// <dialog> nativo: Esc, trampa de foco y backdrop sin dependencias.
export default function Modal({
  open,
  onClose,
  title,
  size = "md",
  footer,
  children,
}) {
  const ref = useRef(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const d = ref.current;
    if (!d) return;
    if (open && !d.open) d.showModal();
    if (!open && d.open) d.close();
  }, [open]);

  return (
    <dialog
      ref={ref}
      onClose={onClose}
      onMouseDown={(e) => e.target === ref.current && onClose()}
      className={`m-auto w-[calc(100%-2rem)] ${sizes[size]} rounded-xl bg-white p-0 shadow-2xl backdrop:bg-slate-900/50`}
    >
      {open && (
        <div className="flex max-h-[90vh] flex-col">
          <header className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 px-5 py-4">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-800">{title}</h2>
            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              aria-label="Cerrar"
              className="rounded p-1 text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100"
            >
              <X size={18} />
            </button>
          </header>
          <div className="overflow-y-auto px-5 py-4">{children}</div>
          {footer && (
            <footer className="flex justify-end gap-2 border-t border-slate-200 px-5 py-3">
              {footer}
            </footer>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </dialog>
  );
}




//==========================================
// FILE: frontend\src\components\ui\Toast.jsx
//==========================================
// frontend/src/components/ui/Toast.jsx
import {
  createContext,
  useCallback,
  useContext,
  useMemo,
  useState,
} from "react";

const Ctx = createContext(null);
export const useToast = () => useContext(Ctx);
const tone = {
  success: "bg-emerald-600",
  error: "bg-red-600",
  info: "bg-sky-600",
  warning: "bg-amber-500",
};

export function ToastProvider({ children }) {
  const [items, setItems] = useState([]);
  const push = useCallback((type, msg) => {
    const id = crypto.randomUUID();
    setItems((s) => [...s, { id, type, msg }]);
    setTimeout(() => setItems((s) => s.filter((i) => i.id !== id)), 3500);
  }, []);
  const api = useMemo(
    () => ({
      success: (m) => push("success", m),
      error: (m) => push("error", m),
      info: (m) => push("info", m),
      warning: (m) => push("warning", m),
    }),
    [push],
  );
  return (
    <Ctx.Provider value={api}>
      {children}
      <div
        className="fixed right-4 top-4 z-100 flex flex-col gap-2"
        role="status"
        aria-live="polite"
      >
        {items.map((i) => (
          <div
            key={i.id}
            className={`${tone[i.type]} max-w-sm rounded-lg px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white shadow-lg`}
          >
            {i.msg}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </Ctx.Provider>
  );
}




//==========================================
// FILE: frontend\src\context\AuthContext.jsx
//==========================================
// frontend/src/context/AuthContext.jsx
import { createContext, useContext } from "react";
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { api } from "../api/client.js";

const AuthContext = createContext(null);

export function AuthProvider({ children }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();

  const { data: user, isLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["me"],
    queryFn: () => api.get("/auth/me").then((r) => r.data.data),
    staleTime: Infinity,
    retry: false,
  });

  const login = async (credentials) => {
    const { data } = await api.post("/auth/login", credentials);
    qc.setQueryData(["me"], data.data);
  };

  const logout = async () => {
    await api.post("/auth/logout");
    // Notifica de forma determinista que la sesión terminó.
    // setQueryData es más predecible que qc.clear(): escribe null y notifica
    // a todos los suscriptores de ["me"] al instante.
    qc.setQueryData(["me"], null);
    // Limpia el resto del caché de la sesión anterior.
    qc.removeQueries({ queryKey: ["turno"] });
    qc.removeQueries({ queryKey: ["empresa"] });
    qc.removeQueries({ queryKey: ["dashboard"] });
    qc.removeQueries({ queryKey: ["vehiculos"] });
    qc.removeQueries({ queryKey: ["usuarios"] });
    qc.removeQueries({ queryKey: ["tipos"] });
    qc.removeQueries({ queryKey: ["tarifas"] });
    qc.removeQueries({ queryKey: ["mensualidades"] });
    qc.removeQueries({ queryKey: ["reportes"] });
    qc.removeQueries({ queryKey: ["ticket"] });
  };

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider
      value={{ user: user ?? null, isLoading, login, logout }}
    >
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export const useAuth = () => {
  const ctx = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error("useAuth debe usarse dentro de <AuthProvider>");
  return ctx;
};




//==========================================
// FILE: frontend\src\hooks\useApi.js
//==========================================
// frontend/src/hooks/useApi.js
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { api } from "../api/client";
import { useToast } from "../components/ui/Toast";

const unwrap = (r) => r.data.data;

export const useGet = (key, url, options) =>
  useQuery({
    queryKey: key,
    queryFn: () => api.get(url).then(unwrap),
    ...options,
  });

// silent: el formulario muestra el error en línea, sin toast.
export function useApiMutation(
  fn,
  { invalidate = [], onSuccess, silent = false } = {},
) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const toast = useToast();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (v) => fn(v).then(unwrap),
    onSuccess: (data, vars) => {
      invalidate.forEach((queryKey) => qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey }));
      onSuccess?.(data, vars);
    },
    onError: (e) => !silent && toast.error(e.userMessage || "Ocurrió un error"),
  });
}




//==========================================
// FILE: frontend\src\hooks\useEmpresa.js
//==========================================
// frontend/src/hooks/useEmpresa.js
import { useMemo } from "react";
import { useGet } from "./useApi";
import { formatMoney, formatDateTime } from "../utils/format";

const STALE = 5 * 60_000;
export const useEmpresa = () =>
  useGet(["empresa", "me"], "/empresa/me", { staleTime: STALE });
export const useEmpresaConfig = () =>
  useGet(["empresa", "config"], "/empresa/config", { staleTime: STALE });

// Formateadores ya ligados a la moneda y zona horaria de la empresa.
export function useFormat() {
  const { data: c } = useEmpresaConfig();
  const moneda = c?.moneda ?? "COP";
  const tz = c?.zona_horaria ?? "America/Bogota";
  return useMemo(
    () => ({
      moneda,
      tz,
      fmtMoney: (n) => formatMoney(n, moneda),
      fmtDate: (iso) => (iso ? formatDateTime(iso, tz) : ""),
    }),
    [moneda, tz],
  );
}

import { useMutation, useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { api } from "../api/client.js";

// Custom hooks = la capa de "servicios API" del frontend: los componentes no conocen URLs ni axios.

export const useTiposActivos = () =>
  useQuery({
    queryKey: ["tipos-activos"],
    queryFn: () => api.get("/tipos-vehiculos/activos").then((r) => r.data.data),
    staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000, // los tipos cambian poco: 5 min de caché
  });

export const useRegistrarIngreso = () =>
  useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body) =>
      api.post("/movimientos/ingreso", body).then((r) => r.data.data),
  });

export const useCalcularSalida = () =>
  useMutation({
    mutationFn: (placa) =>
      api
        .post("/movimientos/calcular-salida", { placa })
        .then((r) => r.data.data),
  });

export const useConfirmarSalida = () =>
  useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body) =>
      api.post("/movimientos/confirmar-salida", body).then((r) => r.data.data),
  });

// frontend/src/pages/ConfiguracionPage.jsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import {
  Building2,
  SlidersHorizontal,
  Save,
  Upload,
  LocateFixed,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { api } from "../api/client";
import { useApiMutation } from "../hooks/useApi";
import { useEmpresa, useEmpresaConfig } from "../hooks/useEmpresa";
import Card from "../components/ui/Card";
import Button from "../components/ui/Button";
import { Field, FormError, inputCls } from "../components/ui/Field";
import { useToast } from "../components/ui/Toast";

const ZONAS = [
  ["America/Mexico_City", "México (Ciudad de México)"],
  ["America/Cancun", "México (Cancún)"],
  ["America/Tijuana", "México (Tijuana)"],
  ["America/Bogota", "Colombia (Bogotá)"],
  ["America/Lima", "Perú (Lima)"],
  ["America/Guayaquil", "Ecuador (Guayaquil)"],
  ["America/Caracas", "Venezuela (Caracas)"],
  ["America/La_Paz", "Bolivia (La Paz)"],
  ["America/Santiago", "Chile (Santiago)"],
  ["America/Argentina/Buenos_Aires", "Argentina (Buenos Aires)"],
  ["America/Asuncion", "Paraguay (Asunción)"],
  ["America/Montevideo", "Uruguay (Montevideo)"],
  ["America/Panama", "Panamá"],
  ["America/Costa_Rica", "Costa Rica"],
  ["America/Guatemala", "Guatemala"],
  ["America/El_Salvador", "El Salvador"],
  ["America/Tegucigalpa", "Honduras"],
  ["America/Managua", "Nicaragua"],
  ["America/Santo_Domingo", "República Dominicana"],
  ["America/Havana", "Cuba (La Habana)"],
  ["America/New_York", "EE.UU. (Nueva York)"],
  ["America/Chicago", "EE.UU. (Chicago)"],
  ["America/Denver", "EE.UU. (Denver)"],
  ["America/Los_Angeles", "EE.UU. (Los Ángeles)"],
  ["Europe/Madrid", "España (Madrid)"],
  ["Atlantic/Canary", "España (Canarias)"],
  ["UTC", "UTC"],
];
const hhmm = (t) => String(t ?? "").slice(0, 5);
const LOGO_URL = `${import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL ?? "/api"}/empresa/logo`;

function LogoUploader({ tieneLogo }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const toast = useToast();
  const input = useRef(null);
  const [file, setFile] = useState(null);
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState(null);
  const [v, setV] = useState(() => Date.now());
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => () => preview && URL.revokeObjectURL(preview), [preview]);

  function onPick(e) {
    const f = e.target.files?.[0];
    setError("");
    if (!f) return (setFile(null), setPreview(null));
    if (!["image/png", "image/jpeg", "image/gif"].includes(f.type)) {
      e.target.value = "";
      return setError("Tipo de archivo no permitido. Usa PNG o JPG.");
    }
    if (f.size > 2 * 1024 * 1024) {
      e.target.value = "";
      return setError("El archivo excede 2 MB.");
    }
    setFile(f);
    setPreview(URL.createObjectURL(f));
  }

  async function subir() {
    if (!file) return setError("Selecciona un archivo de logo.");
    setBusy(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      const form = new FormData();
      form.append("logo", file);
      await api.post("/empresa/logo", form);
      await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["empresa", "me"] });
      setV(Date.now());
      setFile(null);
      setPreview(null);
      if (input.current) input.current.value = "";
      toast.success("Logo subido y guardado");
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e.userMessage);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  const src = preview ?? (tieneLogo ? `${LOGO_URL}?v=${v}` : null);
  return (
    <div className="space-y-2">
      <FormError message={error} />
      <Field label="Logo">
        <input
          ref={input}
          type="file"
          accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/gif"
          onChange={onPick}
          className="block w-full text-sm file:mr-3 file:rounded-lg file:border-0 file:bg-slate-100 file:px-3 file:py-2"
        />
      </Field>
      {src && (
        <img
          src={src}
          alt="Vista previa del logo"
          className="max-h-20 rounded border border-slate-200"
        />
      )}
      <Button variant="outline" onClick={subir} loading={busy}>
        <Upload size={14} className="mr-1" />
        Subir
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}

function EmpresaCard() {
  const toast = useToast();
  const { data: e } = useEmpresa();
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const { register, handleSubmit, reset } = useForm();
  useEffect(() => {
    if (e)
      reset({
        nombre: e.nombre,
        nit: e.nit,
        direccion: e.direccion ?? "",
        telefono: e.telefono ?? "",
        email: e.email ?? "",
      });
  }, [e, reset]);
  const save = useApiMutation((v) => api.put("/empresa", v), {
    invalidate: [["empresa", "me"]],
    silent: true,
  });
  const onSubmit = async (v) => {
    setError("");
    try {
      await save.mutateAsync(v);
      toast.success("Datos de empresa actualizados");
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.userMessage);
    }
  };
  return (
    <Card
      title="Datos de la Empresa"
      icon={Building2}
      actions={
        <Button loading={save.isPending} onClick={handleSubmit(onSubmit)}>
          <Save size={14} className="mr-1" />
          Guardar
        </Button>
      }
    >
      <form
        onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)}
        className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3"
      >
        <div className="sm:col-span-3">
          <FormError message={error} />
        </div>
        <Field label="Nombre" className="sm:col-span-2">
          <input className={inputCls} required {...register("nombre")} />
        </Field>
        <Field label="NIT">
          <input className={inputCls} required {...register("nit")} />
        </Field>
        <Field label="Dirección" className="sm:col-span-2">
          <input className={inputCls} {...register("direccion")} />
        </Field>
        <Field label="Teléfono">
          <input className={inputCls} {...register("telefono")} />
        </Field>
        <Field label="Email" className="sm:col-span-3">
          <input type="email" className={inputCls} {...register("email")} />
        </Field>
      </form>
      <div className="mt-4 border-t border-slate-100 pt-4">
        <LogoUploader tieneLogo={!!e?.tiene_logo} />
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

function ParametrosCard() {
  const toast = useToast();
  const { data: c } = useEmpresaConfig();
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const { register, handleSubmit, reset, watch, setValue } = useForm();
  const h24 = watch("operacion_24h");
  useEffect(() => {
    if (c)
      reset({
        horario_apertura: hhmm(c.horario_apertura),
        horario_cierre: hhmm(c.horario_cierre),
        operacion_24h: !!c.operacion_24h,
        iva_porcentaje: c.iva_porcentaje ?? 0,
        moneda: c.moneda ?? "COP",
        zona_horaria: c.zona_horaria ?? "America/Bogota",
      });
  }, [c, reset]);

  const save = useApiMutation((v) => api.put("/empresa/config", v), {
    invalidate: [["empresa", "config"]],
    silent: true,
  });
  const onSubmit = async (v) => {
    setError("");
    const body = { ...v };
    if (v.operacion_24h) {
      delete body.horario_apertura;
      delete body.horario_cierre;
    } // los inputs deshabilitados no se envían
    try {
      await save.mutateAsync(body);
      toast.success("Configuración actualizada");
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.userMessage);
    }
  };

  function detectar() {
    const tz = Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone;
    if (!tz) return setError("No se pudo detectar la zona del equipo");
    setValue("zona_horaria", tz);
    toast.info(`Zona detectada: ${tz}. Guarda para aplicarla.`);
  }
  const zonaActual = watch("zona_horaria");
  const zonas =
    ZONAS.some(([z]) => z === zonaActual) || !zonaActual
      ? ZONAS
      : [...ZONAS, [zonaActual, `${zonaActual} (personalizada)`]];

  return (
    <Card
      title="Configuración"
      icon={SlidersHorizontal}
      actions={
        <Button loading={save.isPending} onClick={handleSubmit(onSubmit)}>
          <Save size={14} className="mr-1" />
          Guardar
        </Button>
      }
    >
      <form
        onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)}
        className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2"
      >
        <div className="sm:col-span-2">
          <FormError message={error} />
        </div>
        <p className="rounded-lg bg-sky-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-sky-800 sm:col-span-2">
          Las capacidades de vehículos se configuran en{" "}
          <b>Tipos de Vehículos</b>.
        </p>
        <Field label="Apertura">
          <input
            type="time"
            className={inputCls}
            disabled={h24}
            required={!h24}
            {...register("horario_apertura")}
          />
        </Field>
        <Field label="Cierre">
          <input
            type="time"
            className={inputCls}
            disabled={h24}
            required={!h24}
            {...register("horario_cierre")}
          />
        </Field>
        <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm sm:col-span-2">
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            className="h-4 w-4 accent-brand-600"
            {...register("operacion_24h")}
          />
          Abierto 24 horas
        </label>
        <Field label="IVA (%)">
          <input
            type="number"
            step="0.01"
            min="0"
            max="100"
            className={inputCls}
            required
            {...register("iva_porcentaje")}
          />
        </Field>
        <Field label="Moneda">
          <input
            className={`${inputCls} uppercase`}
            required
            {...register("moneda")}
          />
        </Field>
        <Field
          label="Zona horaria"
          className="sm:col-span-2"
          hint="Elige la zona del país donde opera el parqueadero."
        >
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <select className={inputCls} required {...register("zona_horaria")}>
              {zonas.map(([z, n]) => (
                <option key={z} value={z}>
                  {n}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={detectar}
              title="Detectar zona del equipo"
              className="rounded-lg border border-slate-300 px-3 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50"
            >
              <LocateFixed size={16} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </Field>
      </form>
    </Card>
  );
}

export default function ConfiguracionPage() {
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-6 xl:grid-cols-2">
      <EmpresaCard />
      <ParametrosCard />
    </div>
  );
}

// frontend/src/pages/DashboardPage.jsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { keepPreviousData } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import {
  Car,
  Banknote,
  ParkingSquare,
  Eye,
  PlusCircle,
  Printer,
  ChartBar,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useGet } from "../hooks/useApi";
import { useFormat } from "../hooks/useEmpresa";

import { useTicket } from "../components/tickets/ComprobantePanel";
import { buildTicketHtml } from "../components/tickets/ticketHtml";
import { printHtml } from "../utils/print";
import Card from "../components/ui/Card";
import Badge from "../components/ui/Badge";
import Button from "../components/ui/Button";
import Modal from "../components/ui/Modal";

function StatCard({ icon: Icon, color, title, children }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-4 rounded-xl bg-white p-5 shadow-sm ring-1 ring-slate-200 transition-transform hover:-translate-y-1">
      <div
        className={`flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl text-white ${color}`}
      >
        <Icon size={24} />
      </div>
      <div className="min-w-0">
        <h3 className="mb-1 text-sm text-slate-500">{title}</h3>
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function DetalleModal({ id, onClose }) {
  const { data, isLoading } = useTicket(id);
  const fmt = useFormat();
  const tipo = data?.movimiento.estado === "finalizado" ? "SALIDA" : "INGRESO";
  const imprimir = (w) =>
    printHtml(buildTicketHtml(data, tipo, fmt), {
      widthMm: w,
      title: "Comprobante",
    });
  return (
    <Modal
      open
      onClose={onClose}
      title={`Detalle Movimiento #${id}`}
      footer={
        <>
          {data && (
            <>
              <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => imprimir(58)}>
                <Printer size={14} className="mr-1" />
                58 mm
              </Button>
              <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => imprimir(80)}>
                <Printer size={14} className="mr-1" />
                80 mm
              </Button>
            </>
          )}
          <Button variant="outline" onClick={onClose}>
            Cerrar
          </Button>
        </>
      }
    >
      {isLoading || !data ? (
        <p className="py-4 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">Cargando…</p>
      ) : (
        <dl className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-sm">
          <dt className="text-slate-500">Placa</dt>
          <dd className="col-span-2 font-medium">{data.vehiculo.placa}</dd>
          <dt className="text-slate-500">Tipo</dt>
          <dd className="col-span-2">{data.vehiculo.tipo}</dd>
          <dt className="text-slate-500">Entrada</dt>
          <dd className="col-span-2">
            {fmt.fmtDate(data.movimiento.fecha_entrada)}
          </dd>
          {data.movimiento.fecha_salida && (
            <>
              <dt className="text-slate-500">Salida</dt>
              <dd className="col-span-2">
                {fmt.fmtDate(data.movimiento.fecha_salida)}
              </dd>
            </>
          )}
          {data.movimiento.total != null && (
            <>
              <dt className="text-slate-500">Total</dt>
              <dd className="col-span-2 font-medium">
                {fmt.fmtMoney(data.movimiento.total)}
              </dd>
            </>
          )}
        </dl>
      )}
    </Modal>
  );
}

export default function DashboardPage() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { fmtMoney, fmtDate } = useFormat();
  const [page, setPage] = useState(0);
  const [detalle, setDetalle] = useState(null);
  const { data, isLoading, error } = useGet(
    ["dashboard", page],
    `/dashboard/stats?page=${page}&pageSize=5`,
    { placeholderData: keepPreviousData },
  );

  return (
    <>
      <div className="mb-6 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
        <StatCard
          icon={Car}
          color="bg-brand-600"
          title="Vehículos actuales (por tipo)"
        >
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-5 gap-y-1">
            {(data?.porTipo ?? []).map((t) => (
              <div key={t.id_tipo}>
                <small className="text-slate-500">{t.nombre}</small>
                <div className="text-2xl font-bold">{t.cantidad}</div>
              </div>
            ))}
            {!isLoading && data?.porTipo?.length === 0 && (
              <span className="text-sm text-slate-500">Sin tipos activos</span>
            )}
          </div>
        </StatCard>
        <StatCard icon={Banknote} color="bg-emerald-600" title="Ingresos hoy">
          <div className="text-3xl font-semibold">
            {fmtMoney(data?.ingresosHoy ?? 0)}
          </div>
        </StatCard>
        <StatCard icon={ParkingSquare} color="bg-amber-500" title="Ocupación">
          <div className="text-3xl font-semibold">{data?.ocupacion ?? 0}%</div>
        </StatCard>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-6 xl:grid-cols-3">
        <Card className="xl:col-span-2" title="Actividad reciente">
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b text-slate-600">
                  {["Placa", "Tipo", "Entrada", "Estado", "Acciones"].map(
                    (h) => (
                      <th key={h} className="px-3 py-2 font-semibold">
                        {h}
                      </th>
                    ),
                  )}
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {isLoading ? (
                  <tr>
                    <td colSpan={5} className="py-6 text-center text-slate-500">
                      Cargando…
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ) : error ? (
                  <tr>
                    <td colSpan={5} className="py-6 text-center text-red-600">
                      {error.userMessage ?? "No se pudo cargar el dashboard"}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ) : !data?.actividad?.length ? (
                  <tr>
                    <td colSpan={5} className="py-6 text-center text-slate-500">
                      No hay actividad reciente
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ) : (
                  data.actividad.map((a) => (
                    <tr
                      key={a.id}
                      className="border-b border-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-50"
                    >
                      <td className="px-3 py-2 font-medium">{a.placa}</td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2">{a.tipo}</td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2">{fmtDate(a.entrada)}</td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2">
                        <Badge tone={a.estado === "activo" ? "green" : "gray"}>
                          {a.estado}
                        </Badge>
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2">
                        <button
                          title="Ver detalle"
                          onClick={() => setDetalle(a.id)}
                          className="rounded bg-brand-600 p-1.5 text-white hover:opacity-90"
                        >
                          <Eye size={14} />
                        </button>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))
                )}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
          <div className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-between text-sm">
            <Button
              variant="outline"
              disabled={page === 0}
              onClick={() => setPage(page - 1)}
            >
              Anterior
            </Button>
            <span className="text-slate-500">Página {page + 1}</span>
            <Button
              variant="outline"
              disabled={!data?.paging.hasNext}
              onClick={() => setPage(page + 1)}
            >
              Siguiente
            </Button>
          </div>
        </Card>

        <Card title="Acciones rápidas">
          <div className="grid gap-3">
            <Link
              to="/admin/ingreso-salida"
              className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-brand-600 px-4 py-4 text-white hover:opacity-90"
            >
              <PlusCircle size={18} />
              Registrar Ingreso / Salida
            </Link>
            {user?.rol === "admin" && (
              <Link
                to="/admin/reportes"
                className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-sky-500 px-4 py-4 text-white hover:opacity-90"
              >
                <ChartBar size={18} />
                Generar Reporte
              </Link>
            )}
          </div>
        </Card>
      </div>
      {detalle && (
        <DetalleModal id={detalle} onClose={() => setDetalle(null)} />
      )}
    </>
  );
}
