// frontend/src/pages/VehiculosPage.jsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import { Plus, Pencil, Trash2, Car } from "lucide-react";
import { api } from "../api/client";
import { useGet, useApiMutation } from "../hooks/useApi";
import { useFormat } from "../hooks/useEmpresa";
import Card from "../components/ui/Card";
import DataTable from "../components/ui/DataTable";
import Badge from "../components/ui/Badge";
import Button from "../components/ui/Button";
import Modal from "../components/ui/Modal";
import ConfirmDialog from "../components/ui/ConfirmDialog";
import { Field, FormError, inputCls } from "../components/ui/Field";
import { useToast } from "../components/ui/Toast";

function VehiculoModal({ vehiculo, tipos, onClose }) {
  const toast = useToast();
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const { register, handleSubmit } = useForm({
    defaultValues: {
      placa: vehiculo.placa ?? "",
      id_tipo: vehiculo.id_tipo ?? "",
      color: vehiculo.color ?? "",
      modelo: vehiculo.modelo ?? "",
    },
  });
  const save = useApiMutation(
    (v) =>
      vehiculo.id_vehiculo
        ? api.put(`/vehiculos/${vehiculo.id_vehiculo}`, v)
        : api.post("/vehiculos", v),
    { invalidate: [["vehiculos"]], silent: true },
  );
  const onSubmit = async (v) => {
    setError("");
    try {
      await save.mutateAsync(v);
      toast.success(
        vehiculo.id_vehiculo ? "Vehículo actualizado" : "Vehículo registrado",
      );
      onClose();
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e.userMessage);
    }
  };
  return (
    <Modal
      open
      onClose={onClose}
      title={vehiculo.id_vehiculo ? "Editar Vehículo" : "Nuevo Vehículo"}
      footer={
        <>
          <Button variant="outline" onClick={onClose}>
            Cancelar
          </Button>
          <Button type="submit" form="veh-form" loading={save.isPending}>
            Guardar
          </Button>
        </>
      }
    >
      <form
        id="veh-form"
        onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)}
        className="space-y-4"
      >
        <FormError message={error} />
        <Field label="Placa">
          <input
            className={`${inputCls} uppercase`}
            required
            maxLength={10}
            {...register("placa")}
          />
        </Field>
        <Field label="Tipo">
          <select className={inputCls} required {...register("id_tipo")}>
            <option value="">Seleccione un tipo</option>
            {tipos.map((t) => (
              <option key={t.id_tipo} value={t.id_tipo}>
                {t.nombre}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </Field>
        <Field label="Color">
          <input className={inputCls} required {...register("color")} />
        </Field>
        <Field label="Modelo">
          <input className={inputCls} {...register("modelo")} />
        </Field>
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
}

function HistorialModal({ vehiculo, onClose }) {
  const { fmtMoney, fmtDate } = useFormat();
  const { data = [], isLoading } = useGet(
    ["vehiculos", "historial", vehiculo.id_vehiculo],
    `/vehiculos/${vehiculo.id_vehiculo}/historial`,
  );
  return (
    <Modal
      open
      onClose={onClose}
      size="lg"
      title={`Historial del Vehículo ${vehiculo.placa}`}
      footer={
        <Button variant="outline" onClick={onClose}>
          Cerrar
        </Button>
      }
    >
      <div className="overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
          <thead>
            <tr className="border-b text-slate-600">
              {["#Mov", "Entrada", "Salida", "Estado", "Total", "Pagos"].map(
                (h) => (
                  <th key={h} className="px-3 py-2 font-semibold">
                    {h}
                  </th>
                ),
              )}
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {isLoading ? (
              <tr>
                <td colSpan={6} className="py-6 text-center text-slate-500">
                  Cargando...
                </td>
              </tr>
            ) : data.length === 0 ? (
              <tr>
                <td colSpan={6} className="py-6 text-center text-slate-500">
                  Sin registros
                </td>
              </tr>
            ) : (
              data.map((m) => {
                const vuelto =
                  Number(m.total_recibido) - Number(m.total_pagado);
                return (
                  <tr
                    key={m.id_movimiento}
                    className="border-b border-slate-100"
                  >
                    <td className="px-3 py-2">{m.id_movimiento}</td>
                    <td className="px-3 py-2">{fmtDate(m.fecha_entrada)}</td>
                    <td className="px-3 py-2">
                      {m.fecha_salida ? fmtDate(m.fecha_salida) : "-"}
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-3 py-2">
                      <Badge tone={m.estado === "activo" ? "green" : "blue"}>
                        {m.estado}
                      </Badge>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-3 py-2">
                      {m.total_a_pagar ? fmtMoney(m.total_a_pagar) : "-"}
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-3 py-2">
                      {m.pagos > 0 ? (
                        <>
                          {fmtMoney(m.total_pagado)} ({m.pagos})
                          {vuelto > 0 && (
                            <div className="text-xs text-emerald-600">
                              Vuelto: {fmtMoney(vuelto)}
                            </div>
                          )}
                        </>
                      ) : (
                        "-"
                      )}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })
            )}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

export default function VehiculosPage() {
  const toast = useToast();
  const { fmtDate } = useFormat();
  const { data = [], isLoading } = useGet(["vehiculos", "lista"], "/vehiculos");
  const { data: tipos = [] } = useGet(
    ["tipos", "activos"],
    "/tipos-vehiculos/activos",
  );
  const [filtroTipo, setFiltroTipo] = useState("");
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(null);
  const [historial, setHistorial] = useState(null);
  const [toDelete, setToDelete] = useState(null);
  const del = useApiMutation((id) => api.delete(`/vehiculos/${id}`), {
    invalidate: [["vehiculos"]],
    onSuccess: () => {
      toast.success("Vehículo eliminado");
      setToDelete(null);
    },
  });

  const rows = filtroTipo
    ? data.filter((v) => String(v.id_tipo) === filtroTipo)
    : data;
  const columns = [
    {
      key: "placa",
      header: "Placa",
      render: (v) => (
        <button
          className="font-medium text-brand-600 hover:underline"
          onClick={() => setHistorial(v)}
        >
          {v.placa}
        </button>
      ),
    },
    { key: "tipo", header: "Tipo" },
    { key: "color", header: "Color" },
    { key: "modelo", header: "Modelo" },
    {
      key: "fecha_registro",
      header: "Fecha Registro",
      render: (v) => fmtDate(v.fecha_registro),
    },
    {
      key: "estado",
      header: "Estado",
      render: (v) => (
        <Badge tone={v.estado === "activo" ? "green" : "gray"}>
          {v.estado === "activo" ? "Activo" : "Inactivo"}
        </Badge>
      ),
    },
    {
      key: "acciones",
      header: "Acciones",
      sortable: false,
      searchable: false,
      render: (v) => (
        <div className="flex gap-1">
          <button
            title="Editar"
            onClick={() => setEditing(v)}
            className="rounded bg-sky-500 p-1.5 text-white hover:bg-sky-600"
          >
            <Pencil size={14} />
          </button>
          <button
            title="Eliminar"
            onClick={() => setToDelete(v)}
            className="rounded bg-red-500 p-1.5 text-white hover:bg-red-600"
          >
            <Trash2 size={14} />
          </button>
        </div>
      ),
    },
  ];

  return (
    <>
      <Card
        title="Gestión de Vehículos"
        icon={Car}
        actions={
          <Button onClick={() => setEditing({})}>
            <Plus size={16} className="mr-1" />
            Nuevo Vehículo
          </Button>
        }
      >
        <DataTable
          columns={columns}
          rows={rows}
          loading={isLoading}
          defaultSort={{ key: "fecha_registro", dir: "desc" }}
          toolbar={
            <select
              value={filtroTipo}
              onChange={(e) => setFiltroTipo(e.target.value)}
              className={`${inputCls} w-48!`}
            >
              <option value="">Todos los tipos</option>
              {tipos.map((t) => (
                <option key={t.id_tipo} value={t.id_tipo}>
                  {t.nombre}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          }
        />
      </Card>
      {editing && (
        <VehiculoModal
          vehiculo={editing}
          tipos={tipos}
          onClose={() => setEditing(null)}
        />
      )}
      {historial && (
        <HistorialModal
          vehiculo={historial}
          onClose={() => setHistorial(null)}
        />
      )}
      <ConfirmDialog
        open={!!toDelete}
        title="Eliminar vehículo"
        confirmText="Eliminar"
        loading={del.isPending}
        message={`¿Eliminar el vehículo ${toDelete?.placa}?`}
        onConfirm={() => del.mutate(toDelete.id_vehiculo)}
        onClose={() => setToDelete(null)}
      />
    </>
  );
}

// frontend/src/utils/barcode.js
import JsBarcode from "jsbarcode";

// Code128 como SVG en string (se incrusta en el ticket estático).
export function barcodeSvg(text) {
  try {
    const svg = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "svg");
    JsBarcode(svg, text, {
      format: "CODE128",
      displayValue: false,
      height: 40,
      margin: 0,
    });
    return svg.outerHTML;
  } catch {
    return "";
  }
}




//==========================================
// FILE: frontend\src\utils\download.js
//==========================================
// frontend/src/utils/download.js
import api from "../api/client";
export async function descargar(url, nombre) {
  const r = await api.get(url, { responseType: "blob" });
  const a = document.createElement("a");
  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(r.data);
  a.download = nombre;
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  a.remove();
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href), 1000);
}




//==========================================
// FILE: frontend\src\utils\format.js
//==========================================
// frontend/src/utils/format.js
// Reemplazo de datetime.js (~170 líneas). El backend entrega fechas ISO en UTC ("...Z") y
// aquí solo se muestran en la zona de la empresa con la API nativa Intl.
export const formatMoney = (n, currency = "COP") =>
  new Intl.NumberFormat("es-CO", {
    style: "currency",
    currency,
    maximumFractionDigits: 0,
  }).format(Number(n ?? 0));

export const formatDateTime = (iso, timeZone = "America/Bogota") =>
  iso
    ? new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-CO", {
        dateStyle: "short",
        timeStyle: "short",
        timeZone,
      }).format(new Date(iso))
    : "";




//==========================================
// FILE: frontend\src\utils\print.js
//==========================================
// frontend/src/utils/print.js
// `title` debe ser una constante del código, nunca un dato del usuario (se inserta sin escapar).
export function ticketDocument(html, { widthMm = 80, title = "Ticket" } = {}) {
  return `<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>${title}</title><style>
    @page{size:${widthMm}mm auto;margin:3mm}
    body{width:${widthMm - 6}mm;margin:0;font:11px Arial,sans-serif;color:#000}
    hr{border:0;border-top:1px dashed #999;margin:6px 0}
    img{display:block;margin:0 auto 6px;max-width:100%;max-height:60px}
    svg{display:block;margin:6px auto 0;max-width:100%}
    .c{text-align:center}.b{font-weight:bold}.row{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px}
  </style></head><body>${html}</body></html>`;
}

export async function printHtml(html, opts = {}) {
  const iframe = document.createElement("iframe");
  iframe.style.cssText =
    "position:fixed;right:0;bottom:0;width:0;height:0;border:0";
  document.body.appendChild(iframe);

  const doc = iframe.contentWindow.document;
  doc.open();
  doc.write(ticketDocument(html, opts));
  doc.close();

  const win = iframe.contentWindow;
  const imgs = [...doc.images].filter((i) => !i.complete);

  await Promise.race([
    Promise.all(
      imgs.map(
        (i) =>
          new Promise((r) => {
            i.onload = i.onerror = r;
          }),
      ),
    ),
    new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 2500)),
  ]);

  const limpiar = () => {
    if (iframe.parentNode) iframe.remove();
  };
  win.onafterprint = limpiar;

  // Pausa breve para que el navegador pinte el documento antes de imprimir.
  // Sin esto, algunos navegadores ignoran print() sobre iframes ocultos.
  setTimeout(() => {
    try {
      win.focus();
      win.print();
    } catch {
      // El navegador puede bloquear print() si no viene de un gesto de usuario.
      // En ese caso, el usuario puede reimprimir con los botones del modal.
    }
  }, 50);

  setTimeout(limpiar, 60_000);
}




//==========================================
// FILE: frontend\src\utils\reportePdf.js
//==========================================
// frontend/src/utils/reportePdf.js
export async function generarPdf({ empresa, filtros, rows, resumen, fmt }) {
  const [{ jsPDF }, { default: autoTable }] = await Promise.all([
    import("jspdf"),
    import("jspdf-autotable"),
  ]);
  const doc = new jsPDF({ orientation: "landscape", unit: "pt", format: "a4" });
  const W = doc.internal.pageSize.getWidth();
  const AZUL = [26, 35, 126];
  const k = resumen.kpis;

  doc.setFillColor(...AZUL);
  doc.rect(0, 0, W, 60, "F");
  doc.setTextColor(255);
  doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold");
  doc.setFontSize(16);
  doc.text("Reporte de Movimientos - ParkSystem", 24, 38);
  doc.setFontSize(10);
  doc.text(`Empresa: ${empresa?.nombre ?? ""}`, W - 280, 24);
  doc.text(`Generado: ${fmt.fmtDate(new Date().toISOString())}`, W - 280, 40);

  doc.setTextColor(0);
  doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal");
  doc.setFontSize(11);
  const sub = [
    `Rango: ${filtros.desde} a ${filtros.hasta}`,
    filtros.tipo && `Tipo: ${filtros.tipo}`,
    filtros.estado && `Estado: ${filtros.estado}`,
    filtros.placa && `Placa: ${filtros.placa.toUpperCase()}`,
  ]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join("  |  ");
  doc.text(sub, 24, 80);

  const estilo = {
    styles: { fontSize: 10, cellPadding: 6 },
    headStyles: { fillColor: AZUL, textColor: 255, fontStyle: "bold" },
  };
  autoTable(doc, {
    ...estilo,
    startY: 95,
    head: [["Indicador", "Valor"]],
    tableWidth: 360,
    body: [
      ["Ingresos totales", fmt.fmtMoney(k.ingresos)],
      ["Parqueo", fmt.fmtMoney(k.ingresosMov)],
      ["Mensualidades", fmt.fmtMoney(k.ingresosMens)],
      ["Tickets", String(k.tickets)],
      ["Promedio ticket", fmt.fmtMoney(k.promedioTicket)],
    ],
  });
  const m = Object.fromEntries(
    resumen.porMetodo.map((x) => [x.metodo_pago, x.total]),
  );
  autoTable(doc, {
    ...estilo,
    startY: doc.lastAutoTable.finalY + 10,
    tableWidth: 360,
    head: [["Efectivo", "Tarjeta", "QR", "Transferencia"]],
    styles: { ...estilo.styles, halign: "right" },
    body: [
      [
        fmt.fmtMoney(m.efectivo ?? 0),
        fmt.fmtMoney(m.tarjeta ?? 0),
        fmt.fmtMoney(m.QR ?? 0),
        fmt.fmtMoney(m.transferencia ?? 0),
      ],
    ],
  });

  autoTable(doc, {
    ...estilo,
    startY: doc.lastAutoTable.finalY + 14,
    styles: { fontSize: 9, cellPadding: 5 },
    alternateRowStyles: { fillColor: [245, 247, 255] },
    head: [
      [
        "#Mov",
        "Placa",
        "Tipo",
        "Entrada",
        "Salida",
        "Estado",
        "Total",
        "Efectivo",
        "Tarjeta",
        "QR",
      ],
    ],
    body: rows.map((r) => [
      r.id_movimiento,
      r.placa,
      r.tipo,
      fmt.fmtDate(r.fecha_entrada),
      r.fecha_salida ? fmt.fmtDate(r.fecha_salida) : "-",
      String(r.estado).toUpperCase(),
      r.total_a_pagar != null ? fmt.fmtMoney(r.total_a_pagar) : "-",
      fmt.fmtMoney(r.efectivo),
      fmt.fmtMoney(r.tarjeta),
      fmt.fmtMoney(r.qr),
    ]),
    columnStyles: {
      6: { halign: "right" },
      7: { halign: "right" },
      8: { halign: "right" },
      9: { halign: "right" },
    },
    didDrawPage: () => {
      doc.setFontSize(9);
      doc.setTextColor(140);
      doc.text(
        `Página ${doc.internal.getNumberOfPages()}`,
        W - 80,
        doc.internal.pageSize.getHeight() - 12,
      );
    },
  });
  doc.setFontSize(11);
  doc.setTextColor(0);
  doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold");
  doc.text(
    `Movimientos: ${rows.length}   |   Total facturado: ${fmt.fmtMoney(rows.reduce((a, r) => a + Number(r.total_a_pagar || 0), 0))}`,
    24,
    doc.lastAutoTable.finalY + 24,
  );
  doc.save(`reporte_movimientos_${filtros.desde}_${filtros.hasta}.pdf`);
}

// FILE: frontend\src\utils\ymd.js
//==========================================
// frontend/src/utils/ymd.js
export const fmtYmd = (s) => (s ? s.split("-").reverse().join("/") : "");
export const hoyYmd = (tz) =>
  new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", { timeZone: tz }).format(new Date());
export const addDaysYmd = (s, n) => {
  const [y, m, d] = s.split("-").map(Number);
  return new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d + n)).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
};
export function mesYmd(s) {
  const [y, m] = s.split("-").map(Number);
  const mm = String(m).padStart(2, "0");
  return [
    `${y}-${mm}-01`,
    `${y}-${mm}-${String(new Date(Date.UTC(y, m, 0)).getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`,
  ];
}
export const qs = (o) =>
  new URLSearchParams(
    Object.entries(o).filter(
      ([, v]) => v !== undefined && v !== null && v !== "",
    ),
  ).toString();
